You always signed your letters “Your ever well-wisher.” Your spiritual **friendship** is the rarest of gems, and it behooves all of us to appreciate, revere, and guard it as our life’s dearest treasure. We here at the San Diego temple love you very much and feel forever grateful for everything you have given us. We pray that we may be able to eternally serve you to our utmost ability.

Your devoted servants at New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California, USA.

## New Hastināpura

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Vyāsa-pūjā offerings are meant for glorifying you. They are not meant for venting grievances. How do we glorify you with our words? The pure devotee is beyond the glory built only on words. The Absolute Truth cannot be accessed just through words but through living words, acts of devotion. As disciples and grand-disciples in your family, we can sincerely glorify your heart and soul by glorifying your movement. We can move forward in this movement and reach you, our spiritual master, only when we follow in your footsteps. That is the real glorification. But those who take religious life for granted will not be able to see the footprints of the truth and will end up venting grievances.

A person’s footprints are at the lowest place but also the most significant position, the foundation. The footprints represent the basis of spiritual life and the fundamental principle defining a person. When we see the lotus footprints of Kṛṣṇa, all the signs are visible on the soles of His feet. Similarly, we can also see your signs, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by following your example.

While wandering in a corner of the forest, the *gopīs* saw Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s footprints, which appeared mixed with Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī’s. Seeing these footprints perturbed them greatly, and they declared that surely Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī must have worshiped Kṛṣṇa with unusual excellence, since She had been privileged to associate with Him in seclusion. \[*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 10:30, Summary\]

We are privileged to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and through pure-hearted service our footprints can also mix with yours. When we recognize that your footprints are in our hearts, we will be able to follow in your footsteps. If we don’t recognize your footprints in our heart, we are choosing to place ours in the dust of the material desert.

When we want to follow you truthfully, we must first understand and practice the basic ABC principles of spiritual life. You have explained in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.17.41: “If a *brāhmaṇa* is not truthful, all his claims as a *brāhmaṇa* at once become null and void. If a *sannyāsī* is illicitly connected with women, all his claims as a *sannyāsī* at once become false.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a living example of what it means to be a human being, what it means to be a *brāhmaṇa,* a *sannyāsī,* and a guru. A human being is conscious about spirit, of which he is a part. A *brāhmaṇa* is conscious about spiritual knowledge, of which he is a servant. A *sannyāsī* is conscious about austerity, of which he is a master. A guru is conscious about teaching the Absolute Truth, of which he is a student.

Now the clock has clicked into the next millennium. Do we know what is right and what is wrong? In the deep darkness of Kali-yuga the only light to guide us is the path traversed by the pure devotees. Do we know that to truly love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, means to love one another? It means learning from our faults, trying to correct them, and working together to push on Lord Caitanya’s mission.

Do we really know that we are not the body? Do we realize that this movement is your transcendental body? At least we should sacrifice our useless flesh and bones, full of lust, envy, and greed, to take our place in the living body of your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. This is how we will make your mission successful. We have to be very serious to become living truth so that we can live the clear vision of you, our spiritual father. This is the real glorification of Vyāsa-pūjā. Only in this way can we truly honor you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you know the direct path to the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the source of our life and love.

Your eternal servants at New Hastināpura Dhāma, Potomac, Maryland, USA.  
(written by Līlā-mañjarī-devī dāsī)

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON and the Golden Age of Lord Caitanya!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Your Divine Grace wanted the ISKCON Berkeley center to develop as a most important educational center in this transcendental movement of Lord Caitanya. Your pure desire is slowly but surely coming to pass. You declared that the Bhaktivedanta Institute is the most important preaching arm of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. About two years ago, your servants in the Bhaktivedanta Institute got involved with the management of New Jagannātha Purī Dhāma. Now we have been given the task of developing this place for your transcendental satisfaction. Back in 1967, almost as soon as you started your ISKCON, you invited Lord Jagannātha to manifest Himself and bless us in the Bay Area. Since then you have changed the lives of so many souls in the Bay Area. By worshiping the most wonderful deities of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Their Lordships Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Lady Subhadrā, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, and Your Divine Grace, we are sure that Lord Jagannātha will certainly send all His servants back to this temple, so that preaching from this temple will certainly expand as per your divine plans.

These servants will certainly include all kinds of devotees. Some of them will maintain all the temple programs you have given us. Others will certainly develop the tools necessary for taking this movement into the academic arena. When your vision for New Jagannātha Purī gradually manifests, your glories will expand into new intellectual and scholarly realms. As Kṛṣṇa is expanding constantly, so are your glories expanding constantly.

You came into this world in 1896, enacted your manifest pastimes for eighty-one years, and continue to guide countless conditioned souls through your most authoritative Bhaktivedanta purports. The devotees in this movement are maintained and nourished by these purports, which are your transcendental ecstasies. The Golden Age of Lord Caitanya within Kali-yuga will last as long as conditioned souls take shelter of your books. Your Divine Grace has declared this. Your books will be the law books for mankind for the next ten thousand years. One can actually associate with Your Divine Grace by reading your divine books. Please let us take shelter of your books more. Please empower us to increase the distribution of your books dramatically in the San Francisco Bay area. Someone might think this will be very difficult to achieve, but we have firm faith that the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya, as embodied in your ISKCON (i.e., your body), is like the waxing moon. It will expand like anything in the coming years. Eventually everyone will become a *harijana*.

We fully expect that the new millennium will be the millennium of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. We read in a Vyāsa-pūjā offering some years ago that the Golden Age of Lord Caitanya started in the five hundredth year after Lord Caitanya’s appearance—that means the Golden Age started in 1986. I think it took at least five hundred years for Christianity to get popular, so history will no doubt repeat itself in the case of Lord Caitanya’s movement. Naturally, as Lord Caitanya’s glories become known, your place as Lord Caitanya’s foremost general will also become known. Millions of people will assemble to chant your glories. At that time, unprecedented peace and tranquility will descend upon the earth. Humanity is realizing that it is suffering; but they don’t know why, and they don’t know how they can transform the face of the earth by supporting and participating in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya and Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to remain your eternal servants and to increase our commitment to preaching the glories of Lord Caitanya and the glories of Your Divine Grace.

Your servants at New Jagannātha Purī Dhāma, ISKCON Berkeley.

## New Naimiṣāraṇya

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. You are liberating the fallen souls by your causeless mercy. Millions of conditioned souls wander in the material world, but only a few meet a pure devotee, by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. And by the pure devotee’s grace the fallen souls can easily cross the ocean of material miseries:

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**“According to their karma, all living entities are wandering throughout the entire universe. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems, and some are going down into the lower planetary systems. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā*** **19.151)**

One has to cultivate the creeper of devotional service by the watering process, which is to chant the *mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. When the creeper begins to grow, one has to take special care to check any weeds that might kill the creeper. These unwanted plants appear in the form of the ten offenses to the holy name.

For the sincere devotee following the footprints you left behind by your mercy, the way back home, back to Godhead, is safe and secure. You are distributing freely the nectar of this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. In a letter written in September of 1972, you revealed your unlimited causeless mercy upon the fallen conditioned souls: “The spiritual master accepts the sinful activities of his disciples from the first initiation. I may give initiation very easily, but what can I do? I am prepared to go to hell for the service of Lord Caitanya.”

Praying at your lotus feet, we offer our eternal obeisances again and again.

Your eternal servants at New Naimiṣāraṇya, Miami, Florida, USA.

## New Nīlācala

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

We feel ourselves humbled and ever grateful to have been given a place at your feet in the Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya-sampradāya. You have proven the heroism of the true Vaiṣṇava, breaking the boundaries of history and grace, shining forth the irrefutable glory of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

It has been analyzed that the commitment of the spiritual master to his disciples can be understood from three factors: from the degree of responsibility (measured in time) taken to train the disciples, the amount of risk (in sacrificing personal comforts) undergone to create and maintain disciples, and the facilities (books, temples, and Deities) furnished by the guru for the advancement and deliverance of his disciples. By these criteria, your preeminent position among all spiritual masters is supremely evident. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are totally convinced of your abiding commitment to your followers. In reciprocation, we simply pray that we may become even a fraction as committed to you and your will.

The spiritual master’s duty and natural characteristic is to be *gaura-vāṇī*, the personification of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s teachings. This is so by dint of both his words and his actions. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can only marvel at your remarkable authentication in this capacity.

In *Śrī Caitanya-candrāmṛta* Śrīla Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī glorifies Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and reveals His magnanimous nature. In one particularly memorable text (78), there appears a list that summarizes all who have been rescued by the Lord’s mercy. The categories are given to clearly establish the magnificence of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s liberality: *pāpīyān*—the most sinful; *hīna-jātiḥ*—those who are without respectable birth or lineage, the lowest born; *duḥśīlaḥ*—those of base character; *duṣkarmaṇām sīmā*—those breaking new ground in sinful activities (pioneers of sin; those in the vanguard of sinful acts); *śvapaca-adhamaḥ*—the vilest of dog-eaters; *satatam durvāsanādhyaḥ*—those constantly enriched by sinfulness; *durdeśa prabhavaḥ*—those born in sinful countries; *tatra vihita-āvāsaḥ*—those continuing to reside in such sinful places; *duḥsaṅgataḥ naṣṭaḥ*—those destroyed by bad company.

The Lord delivered all these—and how perfect a description of the inhabitants of the Western world it is! This is the transcendent vision of Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī in the sixteenth century. Who could have known how accurate these words were to become? The question may arise, How and when did Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu deliver those born in sinful countries who continue to reside there? The clear answer is that He did so, and continues to do so, through Your Divine Grace.

Your servants at New Nīlācala Dhāma, Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.

## New Orleans

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your transcendental loving service. Please allow us to be a small grain of sand in the dust of your lotus feet.

Superficially, the situation of most of the devotees in this *yātrā* resembles yours. You came from India to America, and so have most of us. And as you preached, so must we. We must attain your merciful grace to effectively benefit the conditioned souls. We can do this by being determined and striving to match a little of your intensity in our labors.

Unlike you, most of us are young and married (or are soon to be). Hopefully, as our lives progress we will remain devoted to your mission. We promise to serve your divine lotus feet by maintaining Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Rādhā-Kānta’s temple and engaging all in their service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you worked tirelessly to save the Western world from *māyā.* Please bless us so that we may follow in your footsteps and help save the conditioned souls in this town from the sufferings of Kali-yuga. Please allow us to serve your disciples and help them develop the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement you started. Please free us from personal ambition and help us see everyone with pure spiritual vision. Please help us be fully devoted to your mission, not only ready to sacrifice the fruits of our labor but prepared to dedicate our bodies, minds, and words for Their Lordships’ service.

Please help us chant without offense and teach this chanting throughout the city. We want to distribute your books and *prasādam* for the eternal benefit of the local populace. We pray that we will not become lethargic about your service or become immersed in our own acquisitions.

Please never let us fall into the hellish *māyā* of the tamasic and rajasic *guṇas* but keep us in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We remain ever dedicated to your service, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please accept us.

Sincerely,  
Your loving servants at Saṅkīrtana Purī, New Orleans, Louisiana, USA.

## New Ramaṇa Reti,

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. In great spiritual delight we come together today as a large spiritual family to honor your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā.

New Ramaṇa Reti is a diverse community of about six hundred devotees. Diversity can cause lack of cohesion, and we aren’t always fixed on a unity of purpose. Our varying interests, responsibilities, and philosophical, cultural, and age differences often keep us separate and interacting within smaller groups in the community. Still, some events call us to together, either in service or in our life struggles. They are the potent, uplifting experiences that draw upon us to set our differences aside and become one in interest. In fact, these experiences give us life and energy long after they are over, for there is nothing as powerful as unity in devotion. Such experiences allow us to glimpse the possibility of becoming one in interest with the desire of the Lord. Thus they are important life-forming experiences that bring us closer to our goal and to your desire for our lives.

We at New Ramaṇa Reti came together to build a temple for Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. Then we came together to beautify the temple and your property here. This past year we came together to mourn the passing of close friends and family, to celebrate new life, and to assist those in illness. Each experience strengthens friendships, devotion, and a sense of community.

As much as these community-building experiences have brought us together, though, there is no unifying principle as powerful as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Notably, it is your Vyāsa-pūjā that brings us together as a community as no other event or activity can.

You bring us together, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your ability to unify greatly divergent groups of people is one of your distinguishing devotional qualities, and it served you well in implanting Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the globe. We marvel at your ability to bring together so much diversity in a harmonious offering at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. Such is the spiritual power of your devotion and desire.

You showed us that unity is attainable, but not automatic. It takes patience, diligence, and effort to create unity. Undeviating in purpose, you worked with a variety of personalities, coaching us to stay fixed on the singular goal of pleasing Kṛṣṇa regardless of our differences.

You showed us while you were here—and you give us hope now—that oneness of purpose is possible, even among the members of our diverse group. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the glue in our lives. You are the reason we stay together at all. Your pleasure is the focus of our lives, and we find you in the center of all our thoughts. What you have taught us has formed who we are. It is your purity of purpose, your genuine love for us, that attracts us to your side. When so much is working to tear apart the movement you created and wanted us to spread cooperatively, you keep us in one piece individually and collectively.

And even as we are brought together to face the trials of life or embrace the bliss of service, it is to you whom we turn at every step. It is to your books that we go for solace and enlightenment. It is to your example that we go for strength and encouragement. It is to your memory that we go to lighten our burdens. It is to your *mūrti* that we come year after year to celebrate Vyāsa-pūjā. It is to you whom we come to reveal our hearts in love and prayer.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, hear our prayer. Obeisances to you! All glories to you! Our differences melt away as we gaze upon your golden form and sit together in love for you, chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa and hearing about you. Take us as your property; use each one of us as instruments of your unifying power. In so doing, allow us to glorify you and bring hope and happiness to many, many people. Allow us to make our desire one with your desire, and to work as one unit at Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

Your servants at ISKCON New Ramaṇa Reti, Alachua, Florida, USA.

## New Tālavan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Certainly you are merged in the blissful internal potency of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, your worshipful Lord.

We are trying to work together to fulfill your mission here despite our differences. We are learning to respect one another and not be so puffed up by our slight material acquisitions. We are looking real hard to see one another as pure spiritual beings completely under the Lord’s supervision. We want to love you by showing our appreciation for one another.

We are also realizing one of the most important aspects of Vedic culture: proper respect for bona fide authority, beginning from God and extending down to the *brāhmaṇas,* government officials, property owners, and elders. Especially parents and grandparents, but also children, and even living entities in lower species, also deserve our respect and compassion. Then what to speak of the higher beings? This is the Kṛṣṇa conscious mentality we want to cultivate.

By your grace we can understand the Supreme Personality of Godhead, His spiritual abodes, and the material creations with all its men, demigods, and animals. Thus by your mercy we can transcend this material kingdom and enter into the Absolute Truth’s divine realm. Thus we are forever indebted to you, unlike the Māyāvādī disciples who supposedly transcend their Gurudeva. I cannot imagine missing the nectar you have given. Surely you are the *śikṣā-guru* for the whole world, but especially for the Western world. We are eternally indebted to you because you have unlimitedly increased our opportunity to drink the nectar of the Lord’s divine names, forms, qualities, pastimes, entourage, and paraphernalia.

So we’re trying to please you and pay our eternal debt to you by being Kṛṣṇa conscious, beginning with expressing love, respect, and appreciation for one another—especially for our superiors; older people, *sannyāsīs,* gurus, learned scholars, heads of state, other officials, etc. However, just as when we were young, serving you meant delivering your message to the world, including many superiors. Part of the preaching art is to be able to articulate the Absolute Truth to everyone, whether younger or older, superior or subordinate. Please help us in this regard by appealing to Kṛṣṇa to bestow His grace upon us so that we may become better instruments at your lotus feet.

Your servants at the New Tālavan farm community in Mississippi, USA.

## New Varṣāṇā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I Look Ahead to You**

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
I want to follow you.

The path ahead looms treacherous and narrow,  
Many distractions block my way,  
I wander aimlessly into markets and bazaars,  
They allure me with their bright colors and dazzling lights.

I look ahead to you.  
Your face is grave and urgent,  
You beckon to me, “Come, come!”  
I drop my cheap trinkets and follow again.

The narrow path begins to widen,  
A fiery sun beats down relentlessly,  
I see a shady tree and rest awhile,  
A hand shakes my shoulder gently,  
The sun is sinking fast—I have slept too long.

I look ahead to you.  
Your face is caring but stern,  
You beckon to me, “Don’t delay, don’t delay!”  
I shake off my slumber and walk once more in your shadow.

A gray stone wall now confronts me,  
Thick, cold, and hard—too high to scale.  
It is night now, my darkest hour;  
I struggle pathetically and cry out in pain.

I look ahead to you.  
Your face is kind and reassuring.  
You beckon to me, “Push on, push on!”  
A strong hand lifts me up.  
I drop exhausted to the other side.

The sun rises once more.  
The path ahead seems long and endless.  
I want to give up, go back to that shady tree.  
I sit down dejected, an ocean of self-pity.

I look ahead to you.  
Your face is determined and unyielding.  
You beckon to me, “Be strong, be strong!”  
A hand grabs me and pulls me abruptly to my feet.  
I trudge behind, grumbling but grateful.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
I want to follow you.

I stumble, I fall, I weaken, I hesitate,  
But I look ahead to you.  
Your instructions beckon me,  
They inspire me with strength and hope,  
Tiny glimpses of a transcendental realm,  
A goal worth any struggle.  
I pray to follow always in your footsteps.

I look ahead to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
All glories to you, eternal master and guide!

Your servants at the New Varṣāṇā farm in New Zealand.  
(written by Vimalā-devī dāsī)

## New Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your sweet lotus feet.

You have kindly given us Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vṛndāvana-candra to mercifully watch over us. How many mistakes we have made in Their presence, we are not able to count! Only because of your love for Them and us have we survived countless deviations and falldowns.

Putting the community back on track has taken much more effort than simply following your instructions in the first place! If we can serve your society by demonstrating what not to do, we will be satisfied that we have done some service. Your representatives have kindly accepted our attempts to rectify ourselves by allowing us to host their annual meetings two years in a row! We pray that we can continue to be the servants of your servants.

Your causeless mercy is our only hope to reach the realm of spiritual love.  
Our feeble endeavors would be useless without your compassion from above.

You’ve given knowledge of Kṛṣṇa “as it is” by your complete divine purity.  
Were it not for this, we would surely be lost in ignorant, dark obscurity.

Your pure devotional service was not hindered by a form of flesh,  
and now is not impeded by the lack of that outward dress.

You put your good instructions into law books for ten thousand years,  
and thus are still giving guidance which will vanquish all material fears.

All glories to you, Jagat-Guru, our spiritual master now, today!  
All glories to your full-potent readiness to accept all who will come your way!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, ever well-wisher of each spirit soul,  
Please help us, we beg, to follow all of and only what you have told!

Your insignificant servants in New Vṛndāvana, West Virginia, USA.

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*naiṣāṁ matis tāvad urukramāṅghriṁ*  
*spṛśaty anarthāpagamo yad-arthaḥ*  
*mahīyasāṁ pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekaṁ*  
*niṣkiñcanānāṁ na vṛṇīta yāvat*

**“Unless they smear upon their bodies the dust of the lotus feet of a Vaiṣṇava completely freed from material contamination, persons very much inclined toward materialistic life cannot be attached to the lotus feet of the Lord, who is glorified for His uncommon activities. Only by becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and taking shelter at the lotus feet of the Lord in this way one can be freed from material contamination.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **7.5.32)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda’s pure and unflinchingly devoted servant!

Although you sometimes referred to yourself as the “most unfortunate insignificant beggar,” actually we know you to be quite the opposite of that, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your are the most fortunate devotee because you understand, know, and love Lord Kṛṣṇa, the reservoir of pleasure. Your Divine Grace is the most significant devotee of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and the success of your pure devotional service proves that. How many temples you established for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa is proof! How many hundreds of millions of Vedic scriptures you published and inspired the distribution of is proof! How much mercy you bestow by showering love of God upon so many millions of conditioned souls who are really unfortunate, really insignificant, and really beggars is proof. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is like a great ocean, and it delivers the cure for the disease of forgetfulness of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, which afflicts us with misery. Anyone caught in that ocean’s merciful irresistible undertow is ultimately blessed with reawakening in the ocean of bliss of love of Kṛṣṇa. Everyone in this world is drowning in an ocean of sorrow, and your ocean of mercy is the only remedy to cure our disease of *avidyā*. There is a wonderful *śloka* in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* reminding us of your oceanic mercy of love:

*etāṁ sa āsthāya parātma-niṣṭhām*  
*adhyāsitāṁ pūrvatamair maharṣibhiḥ*  
*ahaṁ tariṣyāmi duranta-pāraṁ*  
*tamo mukundāṅghri-niṣevayaiva*

**“I shall cross over the insurmountable ocean of nescience by being firmly fixed in the service of the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. This was approved by the previous** ***ācāryas,*** **who were fixed in firm devotion to the Lord, Paramātmā, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.23.57)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught by your perfect and inspiring example how to cross safely over the ocean of nescience and enter the ocean of the nectar of devotion. You are the transcendental captain that we desire to be at the helm of Lord Gaurāṅga’s unsinkable ship of love and devotion, guiding us forever. You instructed us to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare and be happy. “Chant these names of God and your life will be sublime,” you said. You so kindly assisted Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu in expanding His white lotus of good fortune by introducing the Hare Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana-yajña* throughout this world of misery and gloom. There is no light, no life, and no bliss without Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Darkness, death, and perpetual suffering are the companions of the faithless, fallen unfortunate fools of Māyā’s prison. Your Divine Grace delivered the treasure of love of God from the storehouse that Śrī Caitanya and Nityānanda plundered by giving freely Their holy names, thus making the unfortunate the most fortunate with the priceless treasure of *kṛṣṇa-prema*.

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* the glories of chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa are proclaimed:

*kaler doṣa-nidhe rājann asti hy eko mahān guṇaḥ*  
*kīrtanād eva kṛṣṇasya mukta-saṅgaḥ paraṁ vrajet*

**“My dear King, although Kali-yuga is an ocean of faults, there is still one good quality about this age: Simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa** ***mahā-mantra,*** **one can become free from material bondage and be promoted to the transcendental kingdom.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **12.3.51)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our debt to you can never and will never be repaid, because what you have given us is the greatest treasure, the matchless gift, and the highest happiness of bliss and love. We desire to serve Your Divine Grace birth after birth, in whatever menial way you wish. We have never met anyone who cared so much about us and about our ultimate success in achieving the blessings of Lord Kṛṣṇa. We pledge our time, energy, life, and soul to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please take us. We are your insignificant and very unfortunate menial servants. We are shameless beggars knocking at your door, asking for shelter at your soft and sweet lotus feet. Everyone loves you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are our loving father and our ever well-wisher. Please bless all your menial insignificant loyal servants here at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandir. Please stay by our side and in our hearts forever, for without Your Divine Grace we are lost. We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please don’t deny our plea.

In closing, we glorify Your Divine Grace with the sweet truth embodied in yet another verse of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* a verse that so nicely describes your wonderful character:

*muniḥ prasanna-gambhīro durvigāhyo duratyayaḥ*  
*ananta-pāro hy akṣobhyaḥ stimitoda ivārṇavaḥ*

**“A saintly sage is happy and pleasing in his external behavior, whereas internally he is most grave and thoughtful. Because his knowledge is immeasurable and unlimited he is never disturbed, and thus in all respects he is like the tranquil waters of the unfathomable and unsurpassable ocean.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.8.5)**

All glories to the topmost swanlike devotee of the most beautiful all-attractive Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva, His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*! We beg to remain your menial servants forever.

Your fallen insignificant children at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandir, Brooklyn, New York, USA.  
(written by Rāmabhadra dāsa)

## Noida

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance! All glories to Your Divine Grace!

For your pleasure, please accept this short updated report of the activities of your ISKCON Noida Temple.

This year saw the introduction of an ISKCON Youth Forum, IYF. Young members of the surrounding community hold a meeting every Saturday night in the temple. The program started almost a year ago, and recently members have started staying overnight and attending the full morning program on Sunday, starting with *maṅgala ārati* and extending up till breakfast *prasādam*. The interest and numbers are steadily increasing.

Under the guidance of His Holiness Lokanāth Swami, we have been planning to develop ISKCON Noida into a predominantly an educational center. And with the development of IYF, many contacts are being made in local schools and colleges. We have a team of preaching devotees who are holding seminars and giving lectures to the students of Noida. Just recently our management and preaching teams received a boost with the addition of Vaiṣṇava dāsa Prabhu, an older gentleman who has taken early retirement to dedicate his life more fully to serving your ISKCON Noida. He has been assisting ISKCON over the past ten years as a competent, reliable, consistent member, and now we look forward to his presence and enthusiasm in developing Noida’s temple preaching in the field of education.

Another aspect of our expansion is land acquisition. The government gives preference and discounted rates to people wishing to establish temples and educational centers, so our proposal to develop an educational temple is being received favorably. We hope to find some suitable land soon and start building an ISKCON-owned facility rather than using a rental property for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī.

Our Food for Life programs, *harināma saṅkīrtana,* and Sunday Feasts are continuing steadily and gradually increasing. The number of guests at our Janmāṣṭamī Festival in 1999 increased to 25,000 from 10,000 the previous year, and our Gītā Appearance festival saw a similar increase in numbers. Our first-ever attempt at organizing a seven-day recitation of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* by His Holiness Rādhā-Govinda Swami met with great success. Over a thousand serious ladies and gentlemen of Noida took advantage of the nectarean and enlivening talks.

We have also recruited devotees who go out to distribute your books daily, so your books are reaching the hands of many people.

In devotee training we still have a long way to go. We are holding festivals, but in cultivation and follow-up we haven’t done as much as we should. It is your mercy that you have given us so much to work on.

We didn’t keep our promise to hold Ratha-yātrā. We wish we could have done that earlier, but now we are committed to do it this year, and by your mercy it will be successful.

It is all going on by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we are very enthusiastic about the way your center is growing. Thank you again for engaging us in this purifying process so that we can all one day obtain the Lord’s special favor.

Your servants at ISKCON Noida, India.

## Nova Goloka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra!! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

Your appearance in this world was essential so we could begin to live. You came only to give us the shelter of your lotus feet, like a loving father protecting his sons. We, your grandchildren, who did not see you in your *vapu* form, have had our illusions demolished by your special bombs in the form of your *vāṇī*. These words of yours passed from your heart through your mouth and entered the ears of our fathers, our gurus, who inspire us by reflecting your qualities and transmitting your *vāṇī* to us.

Your servants at the Nova Goloka farm community in Curitiba, Brazil.  
(written by children in your school here)

## Osafia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Those of us here at our small center within this Israeli Druze village are perhaps getting a little taste of the far greater adventure you undertook for Lord Kṛṣṇa when you arrived in the vast sea of New York’s humanity. The conditions and anxieties your great devotional determination transcended there fill us with amazement and hope. For what could prove more the endless reciprocation the Lord showers on His unalloyed devotee? We have taken on and endeavored so little in your service by comparison (as if there were any comparison), and yet thanks to your mercy and that of so many of your most sincere and steadfast disciples who absorbed what you taught them, we have already been stunned and blessed by some moments of remarkable local outpouring of respect for and interest in the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

So often you showed your disciples what some sincerity, determination, and patience in devotional service can produce by attracting Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, and in great gratitude they have been teaching us this same simple and wondrous thing. The principle is eternal, and we must always savor its power to uplift humanity and dispel our own frustration and anxiety. We here wish to thank you from the bottom of our souls for what you so lovingly proved over and over again to your disciples, and for what they have in turn so powerfully and patiently demonstrated to us. The very pious Druze people here are already benefiting from this mercy and asking more and more about the fathomless wisdom of the *Vedas* and the glorious qualities of Lord Kṛṣṇa. We pray that we might somehow be blessed to gain ever greater faith in this great *paramparā* tradition and to learn properly that most valuable essence in our eternal existence—how to carry it on in its pure, immutable form.

Your eternally grateful servants in Osafia, Israel.  
(written by Param Guru dāsa)

## Osaka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your transcendental books take all the conditioned souls from the darkness of ignorance and bring them to our Saturday programs. Your books have taught us how to search out the Absolute Truth, and we have learned to find happiness in serving Lord Kṛṣṇa rather than trying to be happy without Kṛṣṇa. You have fulfilled the mission of your Guru Mahārāja and have made so many disciples on this earthly planet, and you have kindly sent us sincere, advanced devotees of the Lord. The desire of our spiritual master is the same as that of Your Divine Grace, which is to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone. It is not so amazing that people get books directly from *saṅkīrtana* devotees, but what is amazing is that your books have an uncanny ability to find out sincere people looking for the Absolute Truth! The books themselves go to those people! One of our friends got your book from one of his friends. We have a regular visitor to our Saturday programs who found one of your books in a trash can and didn’t know anything about it but somehow found the word “Kṛṣṇa” interesting. In this way nice guests are coming to our Saturday program. And new guests brings more new guests.

The most important thing for us is to make a sincere effort in devotional service, and this very attitude brings new people to our Saturday program. The development of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Japan seems slow, but I am convinced that if we remain sincere you will shower us with your blessings and thus one day Japan will be flooded with Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.*

Your servants in Osaka, Japan.  
(written by Satsvarūpa dāsa)

## Ottawa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day, the anniversary of your glorious appearance in this world.

Each year when the time arrives to write an offering for you, we try to reflect on the unlimited good fortune you have given us and which you continue to bestow upon the entire world. Because of your firm faith in the instructions of your beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and your full dependence on the mercy and protection of Lord Kṛṣṇa, you left Śrī Vṛndāvana *dhāma* and brought to the Western world the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, as it is. You said that if only one person would accept this message as you purely presented it, you would consider your mission successful. By your purity, not only one but many persons accepted your message. Śrīla Prabhupāda, even now you continue to change the hearts of those who accept and follow your instructions and teachings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly pray that we may also accept and sincerely follow your instructions and teachings and by your mercy become your instruments to pass them on to others, as they are.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that you will always be our “Goal In Life”:

G-uide  
O-verseer  
A-ssociation  
L-eader  
I-nspiration  
N-avigator (captain of our ship)  
L-oving master  
I-nstructor  
F-ather /Grandfather  
E-ver well-wisher

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Ottawa, Canada.  
(written by Śaṅkara dāsa)

## Palankaraya

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

You often mentioned that city life is very hellish, and you predicted that one day the cities would collapse. In our daily life we can feel how strong the bad influence of the so-called modern way of life is in this Age of Kali. Our minds are always in anxiety, and we find insufficient time to cultivate spiritual life in a proper way. You desired, therefore, that we should develop rural communities of devotees where we can appreciate time more and more and where we can learn to become independent of the materialistic way of city life. This will be possible only if we have our own land, where we can maintain cows and learn simply to depend on Lord Kṛṣṇa.

We are the unworthy souls in Palankaraya, Central Kalimantan, Indonesia, now trying to develop a rural farm project, as you desired. We have purchased a piece of land with the purpose of gradually developing it in the direction of self-sufficiency. We have constructed a small building on the land with a simple temple room and altar, where the pictures of Their Lordships are worshiped daily, as the center of the activities there.

Under the guidance of your disciples His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Mahārāja and His Grace Gaura-Maṇḍala-Bhūmi Prabhu we are planting many kinds of vegetables, fruit trees, spices, flowers, etc., without using any chemical pesticide or fertilizer. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is really wonderful to have our own vegetables and fruits from our own land. We are thus able to offer everything we grow to Their Lordships.

Your Divine Grace, it is not easy to change our way of life to the standard of simple living. Therefore please shower us with your matchless mercy so we can remain determined in our humble efforts. Let us learn more and more how to simply depend on the land and cows. We always keep in mind that land and cows are very dear to the Lord, as He showed during His pastimes on earth five thousand years ago. If we are able to live simply and cultivate Kṛṣṇa consciousness along with the vegetables and other crops, we can be sure without any doubt that we are actually pleasing you. And by pleasing you, we can receive your kind mercy. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

Your devotees in Palankaraya, Indonesia.  
(written by Sūrya Locana dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this joyful occasion of your appearance day we wish to offer a small account of the activities of ISKCON Pandharpur, Maharashtra. During the past year we have been working on acquiring more land, which we have now successfully purchased. It connects our existing property to the holy Chandrabhaga River. We are planning to build a palatial temple on her banks, with steps leading down to the water’s edge. The thousands of pilgrims who visit each year to bathe in the Chandrabhaga and then proceed to take *darśana* of Lord Viṭhṭhala will see our temple and be attracted to visit. In this way we are hoping that ISKCON Pandharpur will open up and expand into a popular pilgrimage place.

We have some new additions to our herd of cows. From their milk we take some for the Deities and sell some to maintain the cows. From our wheat crop we are able to supply our neighboring temples, Solapur and Aravade, and from our sugar-cane crop we are able to receive some financial income.

At the moment we are a small team, but hardworking and dedicated. We wish to expand the team to undertake our ambitious temple project. Please be merciful to us and give us the spiritual strength to expand the glories of Lord Viṭhṭhala far and wide.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Pandharpur, Maharashtra, India.

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories unto you!

On this auspicious occasion we are glad to again praise you, but at the same time we are sad not to give you enough satisfactory reports. We have been struggling here to maintain your temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara with only a few of us. At the end of last year some devotees left the temple due to some dispute. Nevertheless, by your unlimited mercy the temple activities are going on for the pleasure of Their Lordships. At the moment the congregation completely maintains our heavy expenses, since we do not have any *saṅkīrtana* devotees free to go out. By Kṛṣṇa’s mercy we have not gone into debt, but we are not getting ahead either.

Still, some basic preaching activities are going on regularly, such as Food for Life and *harināma*. Our congregation regularly receives our bi-monthly newsletter and our French *Bhakti* magazine and attends our main festivals. This year, on July 2nd, we will be holding our ninth Ratha-yātrā festival, which will be, we hope, bigger than ever, since three ambassadors will be inaugurating it. After this major event we hope to be able to at last move to a new place in the core of Paris, so that we can preach more vigorously and have a permanent ISKCON property. This is our main goal, because the rent at our present house is a total waste of energy; paying it is like having our blood sucked from our body. We are convinced that by having a new place in downtown Paris we will be able to bring more conditioned souls to your lotus feet and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara’s lotus feet. We will be able to impart more faith to the weak, conditioned souls, who can only believe in what they see. We are also convinced that, no matter what happens, if we are all united and stick to your instructions, we will be successful. As you indicated, first we should work for quality, then quantity will follow!

With the passing of years, we want to be fixed up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and be able to maintain our *sādhana*. Then, on the strength of good *sādhana,* we will we be able to maintain and even expand the Paris temple. Conversely, poor *sādhana* will only lead us in the other direction and cause disorder and misunderstanding. To be Kṛṣṇa conscious is our prime duty. Your mission has to be built on solid ground. You have set the basics. People come and go, money also, but the philosophy and practices of Kṛṣṇa consciousness should be carved in our hearts, and this is possible only by your unlimited mercy. This is our humble prayer at your lotus feet.

Your servants at the Rādhā-Parisīśvara Mandir, Paris, France.  
(written by Divya Puruṣa dāsa)

## Puncak

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*tomāra hṛdoye sadā govinda-viśrām*  
*govinda kohena—mora vaiṣṇava parāṇ*

“Lord Govinda is always residing within your heart. Indeed, Lord Govinda Himself says, ‘The Vaiṣṇavas are My life and soul.’” (Narottama dasa Ṭhākura, in *Prārthanā*)

It is by your mercy alone that the Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda with Lalitā and Viśākhā, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Lakṣmī-Nṛsiṁha-deva with Prahlāda, Śrī Girirāja, and Śrī Śālagrāma have appeared here in the mountains outside Jakarta to accept the service of all of us, your spiritual children and grandchildren. One of your purposes in establishing ISKCON was to establish “places of transcendental pastimes,” or temples. By your mercy we are trying a little to fulfill your desires in this regard.

We know that because you touched Indonesia with your lotus feet this country will be blessed and people can become devotees, despite whatever problems may be there on the mundane platform. We are happy to inform you that even during these times of trouble, more and more people are coming to the temple, many are taking up Kṛṣṇa consciousness, either as fulltime devotees in the temple or as congregational devotees in their own homes. We have not used any magic formulas or done any mundane business; we have simply tried to carry out your instructions. Among these, one we try to follow strictly is that no one should be allowed to leave the temple without taking *prasādam*. Many Indians and Indonesians are very happy to visit Rādhā-Govinda’s temple again and again. If we have had any small success, we know that it is due only to your mercy, without which we would not be able to achieve anything :

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*

Each day we carry you in procession around Rādhā-Govinda’s temple for *darśana,* recalling how every morning you walked around the Chaitanya Chandrodaya Mandir in Māyāpur many years ago. In this way we feel your presence among us even now, and we pray for your continued guidance.

On this auspicious day of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray that you will continue to bless us so that we may always be engaged in the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, just as you are eternally.

Your aspiring servants at the Rādhā-Govinda tīrtha in Puncak, Indonesia.

## Rāḍhadesh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

When common people asked Your Divine Grace about miracles, you would point to the devotees around you. The real miracle is that people from all backgrounds have become Vaiṣṇavas while Kali was ruling in full swing.

In our ISKCON Rāḍhadesh Dhāma, Belgium, many of us living in this blissful community consider it a similar miracle how this community has not only managed to maintain itself but has steadily grown into a dynamic Kṛṣṇa conscious village. Despite the great internal and external onslaught that ISKCON has withstood since Your Divine Grace disappeared from our material vision, somehow Rāḍhadesh Dhāma has remained apparently untouched.

Not only are about eighty resident devotees enthusiastically engaged in various types of devotional service, but Rāḍhadesh is yearly visited by hundreds of devotees from all over the world and thousands of other people who respond enthusiastically to their first contact with Kṛṣṇa consciousness at the Rāḍhadesh community. Visitors are awestruck when they are brought before Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, who are steadily served by a sincere team of *pūjārīs*. Many people bring back their friends and relatives to show them around, and thus we know that the reputation of Rāḍhadesh has become established without as well as within ISKCON. A construction team consisting mainly of devotees and led by our temple president is steadfastly building a new guesthouse, which will provide first-class facilities for visitors.

Because of the many visitors, Rāḍhadesh is always enlivened by a fresh and dynamic preaching spirit. Devotees preach and distribute your books to the visitors, and twice a week a book distribution team goes out from Rāḍhadesh. Traveling *saṅkīrtana* book distribution is also going on.

A most inspiring achievement is the Rāḍhadesh *gurukula,* which has graduated a dozen children, a few of whom are now ready for secondary schooling in Śrīdhām Māyāpur.

In the future, many thousands of Kṛṣṇa conscious villages will develop all over the world, as predicted by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Still, as we look back over the difficult years since Your Divine Grace left, we feel deeply moved that Rāḍhadesh is blossoming like a fresh lotus flower. The very fact that this has been possible gives us renewed faith and trust that your ISKCON will go on and develop many wonderful devotional projects. All the credit goes to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Without you there would be no ISKCON, there would be no Rāḍhadesh, and there would be no widespread propagation of Lord Caitanya’s mercy by your faithful preachers and servants.

We pray to remain your humble servants in the dust of your lotus feet.

Your servants at ISKCON’s Rāḍhadesh Dhāma, Belgium.

## Rawdon

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

For your pleasure I would like to submit this brief report about our new land project in Rawdon, Quebec, Canada. The land we have acquired is situated at the foothills of the Laurentian Mountains, approximately seventy kilometers north of Montreal. The Laurentians are said to comprise one of oldest mountain chains in the world.

We have been thinking about this project for a long time, but only in January of this year were we able to purchase the land in Rawdon. This is our first step. Unfortunately, at present not many devotees are taking part in this project. But we are confident that gradually, when devotees see this project develop, they will join us. They will understand that natural life—life on our own land—is a very important factor in devotional service.

We are happy that our spiritual guide is His Holiness Bhakti Rāghava Swami. It was his idea, and he was able to organize us, though it was very difficult because we’ve become accustomed to living and working in the city. It was very difficult to raise the money to take our first step, but with Kṛṣṇa’s help and causeless mercy we were able to do it. Now that we have purchased the land, we have to solve a lot of problems, the first of which is exploring it. The lot is quite big, a hundred acres. We couldn’t study it earlier because the snow was very deep, though we did visit several times and study parts of it. Once, when Bhakti Rāghava Swami visited with some Russian devotees, we held a *kīrtana* there and honored *prasādam.* There is a creek to supply the water we’ll need. It is also auspicious that our lot is square, as *Vastu-śāstra* says it must be if we are to be successful. The many trees and rocks on the land will provide plenty of building materials for a temple, housing, and other buildings. Our first project is to construct a temporary temple and a building to house a boutique, a small Govinda’s Restaurant, and some devotees.

We are all looking forward to going to Rawdon and starting the project. Every Sunday we have meetings at the temple where we discuss all the things we must do. I believe that with Kṛṣṇa’s help and your kind mercy we’ll do everything properly. Ultimately, we hope to develop a rural community based on simple living and high thinking, just as you have taught in your books. We hope we can please you by our efforts to fulfill your “unfinished mission,” as you put it—namely to establish an exemplary *varṇāśrama* society to create first-class men and give everyone a chance to grow up spiritually. We are begging for your mercy and service.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for engaging us in your devotional service. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your devotees at Govindapur, the fledgling ISKCON farm project in Rawdon, Quebec, Canada.  
(written by Bhakta Sergey and Jīva dāsa)

## Recife

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we reflect on how mankind was just waiting for you to fulfill Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prediction that the holy names of the Lord would be chanted in every town and village of the world. You lighted up the preaching spirit in all the Vaiṣṇavas, and for this you are accepted by everyone as a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra,* a divine spiritual beam from the Supreme Lord Himself, setting a landmark in the history of Vaiṣṇavism and humankind as well. Gradually, by your grace, the Vedic culture is being established in the whole world. Through hundreds of Hare Kṛṣṇa temples and thousands of devotees and millions of your books, it is being spread everywhere.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for leaving the Rādhā-Dāmodara temple, where you were living in complete spiritual peace, and coming to the West. It was in this place of irreligion that you started a spiritual chain reaction that quickly created thousands of Vaiṣṇavas in every corner of the world. This simply shows that Your Divine Grace had the highest degree of compassion, since you just couldn’t live peacefully in Vṛndāvana when the whole world was suffering for lack of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Just like Prahlāda Mahārāja, you were moved by your compassion for suffering mankind to give them the only remedy, pure devotional service. Thank you for coming, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and especially thank you for giving the basis for the world spiritual revolution—your books.

Your servants in Recife, Brazil.  
(written by Hari Cakra dāsa)

## St. Louis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances into the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā celebrations!

This special day of your Vyāsa-pūjā is a wonderful opportunity for us to more deeply understand how much we are indebted to you, how much you have done for us, and how much you constantly continue to do for us.

Due to our material conditioning and the pressure of our various duties, we may sometimes forget the immense task you performed in saving humanity from degradation down to hellish conditions of life. Our deceitful minds often put the idea into our heads that we are the ones who are so wonderful, performing this or that task. But what could we do without your mercy, which you bestow upon us so freely? This mercy comes to us in the form of the instructions in your books, lectures on audio- and videotapes, your many letters, and the spiritual training you gave to your sincere disciples, who are now educating us on your behalf.

One thing is certain: without your mercy we would only degrade ourselves more and more, day after day. However, due to your natural compassion for us fallen souls of this degraded Age of Kali, you gave us shelter in your movement, ISKCON, which is the best spiritual institution on this planet. It is helping sincere souls make real spiritual progress toward going back home, back to Godhead.

We can see every day that unless we hold tight to your lotus feet we will not receive your mercy, and without your mercy nothing is possible. With your mercy, however, everything is possible. And although time is running quickly and our bodies are getting older every day, by your mercy we can see and feel that there is more and more life in us.

This amazing circumstance is possible because, as you used to say, “Real life begins from the moment of taking up spiritual life.” Therefore, every day that we follow the process of spiritual life you gave us enables us to see how your mercy is saving us, enriching and uplifting us more and more in spiritual understanding and in life itself.

Again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for everything you have done for us and still constantly do for us. Simultaneously, we ask you to help us remain in the shade of your lotus feet, in the shelter of your movement, ISKCON, which is a manifestation of your mercy.

Please help us become better recipients of your mercy, and let us use this gift more and more to make spiritual progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness for your sweet pleasure. Also, please help us learn not only how to receive your gifts but also how to give them to others.

Please bless us with your mercy so that we may remain in the service of your lotus feet.

Your humble and grateful servants in St. Louis, Missouri, USA.

## Saltillo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please let us be instruments of your peace, so that...

Where there is hate, we plant love,  
Where there is doubt, we give faith,  
Where there are untruths, we give truth.

Let us give respect to all *jīvas,* without expecting respect.  
Let us pardon others with all our heart.

Please let your mercy flood our desertlike hearts  
And put out the fire of anger, lust, envy, and jealousy in all of us.

Please let us be as tolerant as a tree, and  
Let only sweet words come from our mouths.  
Please, please let us be filled with all good Vaiṣṇava qualities, and  
Let us be the sincere servants of your servants eternally.

Your servants in Saltillo, Mexico.

## Seattle

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All Glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
most glorious servant of Kṛṣṇa, God.  
I bow down to his feet like a sold out dog,  
praying to please him with all I’ve got.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
the only way to Kṛṣṇa, God,  
to bow before him like a log  
is the human being’s only job!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who holds the perfect rod of God.  
All blessings spring from his glance or nod,  
from his perfect connection with Kṛṣṇa, God!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
pure expansion of Gaura, God,  
showering the nectar of *harinām-saṅkīrtan*,  
to cause all sins to soon be gone!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
perfect servant of Jagannāth,  
guru for the lost, misled, and odd,  
and everyone else from the bottom to top!

All glories to this soul whose words always bless  
all who truly hear with Kṛṣṇa consciousness;  
the surety of his purity destroys the soul’s doubts  
about life’s only goal as Kṛṣṇa’s names he shouts!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda  
for enlightening the lowest,  
this Anubhāvānanda dās,  
who advances the slowest.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
destroyer of demons and defeater of cheaters,  
the leader of leaders is for what his heart’s beating,  
and it is about God alone he’s always repeating!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
for it is him alone who can reveal Kṛṣṇa, God.  
He said, “Follow me and I shall take you where  
all miseries are absent and bliss is all we share!”

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who makes this dumb man write  
words of the highest knowledge,  
the most perfect guiding light.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
My heart cries out in bliss.  
All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
for all glories are truly his!

The all-glorious personality  
is what he can reveal,  
the reservoir of all pleasure,  
the source of all treasure,  
the God the greatest serve with zeal!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
who can bring life to the dead.  
It is I of whom I speak,  
who know what to know is what Prabhupāda said!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
This is perfect wisdom:  
Just obey Śrīla Prabhupāda  
and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa!

For he speaks knowledge which awakens the soul,  
the eternal truth spoken by God,  
what every human being seeks,  
and one’s a dog if one does not!

What is life’s goal cannot be known  
till God’s servant enters our heart,  
for by our desperately hearing his ambrosial words  
our Lord is finally seen when all sins depart.

Sins are darkness performed only by fools,  
for they hear not the Lord who alone speaks life’s rules,  
and life only does begin when we surrender to God,  
and life means pure bliss serving only Prabhupāda!

An absolute oneness is with God and his servants,  
for these servants only want to be in God’s service.  
Yet an absolute difference is also seen,  
for our life and everything depends on Him Supreme.

The planets rest on Him like our feet on the ground,  
and without Him there’s nothing—no sight or sound.  
And without Prabhupāda there’s no way to understand  
how to do anything. So let’s take his hand!

For his hand is in Kṛṣṇa’s, like a child’s in his father’s;  
his love is unbounded, his shelter where God is.  
That God is His names is all we need to know,  
and we’ll know it by chanting them wherever we go!

When we realize God’s His name,  
we have reached life’s perfection,  
where there’s no longer need to fear,  
no hankering or lamentation.

Peace is only reached when we realize this:  
God is His holy names, and everything is His;  
we see everything is His alone to enjoy  
when everything in His service we lovingly employ.

And as soon as we think one thing belongs to us,  
our knowledge turns to ignorance and our love to selfish lust;  
this is our mistake that has made this world hell,  
this is what Prabhupāda came to boldly tell!

Everyone is Kṛṣṇa’s servant,  
and everything belongs to Him,  
and until we realize this,  
our wars will never end!

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s servants in Seattle, Washington, USA.  
(written by Anubhāvānanda dāsa)

## Serbia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

With great happiness in our hearts we celebrate this most auspicious day in the year—a day when all your followers around the world celebrate your Vyāsa-pūjā, your appearance day.

Please allow us to express our gratitude to you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda! For all of us in this country, last year was like a great exam in Spiritual Life School. We found ourselves in many difficult circumstances faced with many obstacles, including war, the loss of our temple, and temporary spiritual weaknesses. Still, your great mercy was always here in the form of your transcendental books, encouraging letters from our leaders, and help from other great devotees around the world.

The question “What is the spiritual master?” is nicely answered in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā* 1.46, purport:

\[The spiritual master\] is like a parent. Without the attentive service of his parents, a child cannot grow to manhood; similarly, without the care of the spiritual master one cannot rise to the plane of transcendental service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most wonderful spiritual master, for hundreds and hundreds of times you showed us the right way to act by your own example. You are the perfect pure devotee who possess the transcendental talent to transmit your knowledge to your followers and other living entities. We are very thankful you possess this talent; otherwise we would be lost in the jungle of the material energy. Even though we still do not possess much transcendental taste for devotional service, we deeply believe we will one day attain this taste in our lifetimes, being blessed by your unlimited mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, from your words and example we drew encouragement to stand up again in spiritual life after being beaten down, and you showed as how to avoid the difficulties around us. Last year we all felt you raining down transcendental nectar upon us, and much new spiritual power infused in the hearts of many devotees here. Our enthusiasm and desire to maintain Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Serbia grow from day to day. Congregational preaching is progressing nicely, but it is still far away from its potential. Still, preaching gives us so much real transcendental happiness. It is our great fortune that we can associate with very advanced devotees who come from time to time to visit and preach to us. With their kind help we progress step by step on this miraculous path of transcendence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your lotus feet are our only shelter! Your teachings are the right guidance to lead us to perfection! Your elevated example is our ideal for a successful life!

We hope that this year we will be able to increase book distribution and other preaching activities in this area.

We love you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in Serbia.

## Solapur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Although we are insignificant and cannot even begin to describe your unlimited glories, we feel very fortunate to have this opportunity to glorify you.

You dedicated your whole life to glorifying the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Now Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa is taking the responsibility to spread your glories by making ISKCON famous all over the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you took so much trouble to spread the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You gave up your business and your family life to go to the West and deliver the conditioned souls. In America you met with many difficulties, but you were never discouraged. You never felt that a lack of money or other material facility was an obstacle. You always had great hope and firm faith in the words of your spiritual master and in the holy names. Through your own example you taught the whole world how to be happy simply through Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were just like a touchstone. Wherever you went you were able to turn even the most degraded, sinful persons into great Vaiṣṇavas.

In the scriptures are many accounts of personalities who were cursed to become snakes, trees, mountains, demons, lizards, etc. For example, Maṇigrīva and Nalakūvara were cursed to become trees, a Vidyādhara was cursed to become a snake, and King Nṛga was cursed to become a lizard. Fortunately for all of them, they were delivered by the touch of the lotus feet or lotus hands of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. We were similarly saved, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We had been rotting in this sinful, material world from time immemorial, sometimes in human bodies and sometimes in those of dogs, hogs, snakes, worms, tigers, trees, insects, etc. But now, by the touch of your lotus feet, the writings by your lotus hands, and the words from your lotus mouth we are getting the opportunity to pursue the real goal of human life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have opened our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. We pray that we shall always remain at your lotus feet.

We are also very grateful that you have sent His Holiness Lokanāth Mahārāja to Solapur to deliver your mercy to the conditioned souls. By his efforts Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacīnandana have appeared in Solapur. We greatly hope that by your mercy and that of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Śacīnandana and Lokanāth Mahārāja, the holy names of the Lord will spread and cure the otherwise incurable disease of material life suffered by the conditioned souls.

We hope to always remain the servants of your servants.

Your servants in Solapur, India.

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*nāca, gāo, bhakta-saṅge kara saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma upadeśi’ tāra’ sarva-jana*

\[Īśvara Purī told Lord Caitanya:\] “My dear child, continue dancing, chanting, and performing *saṅkīrtana* in association with devotees. Furthermore, go out and preach the value of chanting *kṛṣṇa-nāma,* for by this process You will be able to deliver all fallen souls.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā* 7.92)

No one is independent; everyone follows the Leader, who causes all to dance according to His whim. Thrice the Lord of all asserts Himself, forcing a distinction, but the fools cannot grasp it. Now again He has appeared in order to deliver those fools, covered all in gold, the Viśvambhara we cannot see. He leads us now by following, by following best who leads, and each of His steps, though unique and brilliant, still brilliantly conforms. And so it was that the Lord, as a *śiṣya,* did accept His own orders with humility and respect. Not out of any independence but out of simple acquiescence was there thus born the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Though leader we do call Him, and certainly it is so, as follower He protests, “What do I know?” This lesson must be well learnt, never forgotten, for to dance this dance we must follow every step. Even if we be God Himself, we be but a follower in this system of *paramparā* that we must accept. It is not we who set the beat, not we who call the tune; it is just for us to dance as the guru directs us to. Everyone in time, everyone in step, nothing irregular, independent, unapproved. Thus the movement flows from the one to the next, from the Lord down to Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and then our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda. O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, after following them all, what would you say to the self-determined call? To the one who “knows you better” than all the rest, or the one who “only wants to help,” no matter the upset? Or to the one, the one, the one who simply two cannot accept? Or to the kind of zealous spirit that even the baby rejects? We think you would say, “If you want to lead, then follow, and following you shall lead. If you want to show your love for me, then work in harmony. In this field of *saṅkīrtana,* in the world of birth and death, if victory you seek, and freedom too, then follow me and follow true. Just see, I acted not of my own accord or in mutinous plot, but in surrender absolute did I succeed, in thought and word and every deed. So this is the secret that we in *paramparā* share, to find the means to the end, the end of all despair: by taking heed the example set, you’ll lack for nothing and everything you’ll get.”

Caitanya is the Lord, perfect and sweet, writing the score of His *līlā* deep. Yet as *ācārya* also, He set the best example as disciple, then guru. His standard is the test. Everything follows, everyone follows, nothing is original but the Lord Himself. Everything comes from something else. Only envy disagrees, being blind to itself. Oh, what rascaldom it is that cannot see that the guru lives for the sake of the legacy, and that as with the Lord and the *ācāryas* before, it’s Prabhupāda’s example we must adore. And of all the good things that he did best, his sublime faith in guru excelled all the rest. So accepting as order Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī’s request, he traveled the sea and came to the West. As from the Lord’s surrender to Īśvara Purī there was born the *saṅkīrtana* movement’s very own form, now ISKCON exists in just the same way because of Prabhupāda’s wonderfully taking his cue in the play. And things will go on in the future as well, so long as we follow and don’t break the spell, so long as we don’t, by an arrogant conceit, break the flow of sweet mercy from the Lord’s lotus feet.

From your servants at ISKCON Sydney, Australia.

## Tallinn

Our dear spiritual grandfather and instructor Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your lotus feet!

Our words of praise cannot glorify you enough. You brought us real spiritual knowledge, you brought us the holy name and the holy places. You brought light and meaning to our lives. You joined us to Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* pastime. What words would be sufficient to express our gratitude for that?! But still, we do want to thank you on this occasion of your appearance anniversary.

We do want to thank you for sheltering us and giving us inspiration and courage to grow and spread your mission. The last year has been a breakthrough year for us in many ways. By your mercy and by the blessings of your pure representatives, we finally have a functioning temple and we finally have your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* published in the local language. And most important of all, we have finally been put into situations where all of us have had to seek unity despite all diversities. Thank you for testing us and allowing us to continue. Thank you for teaching us to be your family.

Once when a devotee asked what was your desire, you exclaimed that your desire was to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Our part in this great endeavor is very little, but we pray that you provide us with the vision and understanding necessary to fulfill our little part in the attainment of your goal. Please be patient with us and always correct us when we are mistaken. Please accept our lowly attempts to serve you.

Your followers at ISKCON Tallinn, Estonia.

## Tampa Bay

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

Today is your glorious appearance day. This is the day we worship, with the utmost appreciation and gratitude, your appearance on this planet. This was the beginning of our liberation from birth and death.

From your birth till your disappearance in November of 1977, your life was the perfect example of pure devotional life. Her are a few examples of your numerous devotional activities.

As a small child of five you wanted to preach the glories of Lord Jagannātha by having your own Ratha-yātrā in Calcutta.

The demands of college prevented you from regularly worshiping your Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Deities. But one night Kṛṣṇa spoke to you in a dream and complained that you shouldn’t have put Him away and should worship Him again. And you resumed Their worship.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were a good student but you never accepted the professors’ criticisms of the Vedic knowledge. In fact, although you passed your exams you never graduated from Scottish Churches’ College, choosing instead to refuse your diploma to protest the British abuse of India.
